import React from 'react';
import { BrowserRouter as Router, Routes, Route } from 'react-router-dom';
import './styles/global.css';

// РРјРїРѕСЂС‚ СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†
import Home from './pages/Home';
import Catalog from './pages/Catalog';
import Ontology from './pages/Ontology';
import Converter from './pages/Converter';
import Generator from './pages/Generator';
import Situation from './pages/Situation';
import Register from './pages/Register';
import Login from './pages/Login';

function App() {
  return (
    <Router
      future={{
        v7_startTransition: true,
        v7_relativeSplatPath: true
      }}
    >
      <Routes>
        {/* РћСЃРЅРѕРІРЅС‹Рµ РјР°СЂС€СЂСѓС‚С‹ */}
        <Route path="/" element={<Home />} />
        <Route path="/catalog" element={<Catalog />} />
        <Route path="/ontology" element={<Ontology />} />
        <Route path="/converter" element={<Converter />} />
        <Route path="/generator" element={<Generator />} />
        <Route path="/register" element={<Register />} />
        <Route path="/login" element={<Login />} />

        {/* РњР°СЂС€СЂСѓС‚С‹ РґР»СЏ СЃРёС‚СѓР°С†РёР№ - РћР§Р•РќР¬ Р’РђР–РќРћ: РїСЂР°РІРёР»СЊРЅС‹Р№ РїРѕСЂСЏРґРѕРє! */}
        <Route path="/situation/:sitId/:nodeId" element={<Situation />} />
        <Route path="/situation/:sitId" element={<Situation />} />

        {/* РЈРґР°Р»СЏРµРј РґСѓР±Р»РёРєР°С‚С‹ */}
        {/* <Route path="/situation/:id" element={<Situation />} /> в†ђ РЈР”РђР›РРўР¬! */}

        {/* РЎС‚Р°С‚РёС‡РµСЃРєРёРµ СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†С‹ */}
        <Route path="/terms" element={<TermsPage />} />
        <Route path="/privacy" element={<PrivacyPage />} />

        {/* РЎС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Р° 404 */}
        <Route path="*" element={<NotFoundPage />} />
      </Routes>
    </Router>
  );
}

// Р’СЂРµРјРµРЅРЅС‹Рµ РєРѕРјРїРѕРЅРµРЅС‚С‹ РґР»СЏ СЃС‚Р°С‚РёС‡РµСЃРєРёС… СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†

      <p>РЎРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёРјРѕРµ СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†С‹ СѓСЃР»РѕРІРёР№ РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°РЅРёСЏ...</p>
    </div>
  );
}

function PrivacyPage() {

    <div style={{ padding: '2rem' }}>
      <h1>РџРѕР»РёС‚РёРєР° РєРѕРЅС„РёРґРµРЅС†РёР°Р»СЊРЅРѕСЃС‚Рё</h1>

    </div>

}

  return (
    <div style={{
      display: 'flex',

      <h1>404 - РЎС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Р° РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅР°</h1>
      <p>Р—Р°РїСЂР°С€РёРІР°РµРјР°СЏ СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Р° РЅРµ СЃСѓС‰РµСЃС‚РІСѓРµС‚.</p>

}

export default App;

===== D:\projects\teacherTest\teacher-simulator-unified\client\src\components\Button\Button.jsx =====
import React from 'react';
import './Button.css';

const Button = ({ 
  children, 
  variant = 'primary', 
  size = 'md',
  onClick,
  className = '',
  type = 'button',
  disabled = false
}) => {
  const getSizeClass = () => {
    switch(size) {
      case 'sm': return 'btn-sm';
      case 'lg': return 'btn-large';
      default: return '';
    }
  };

  return (
    <button
      type={type}
      className={`btn btn-${variant} ${getSizeClass()} ${className}`}
      onClick={onClick}
      disabled={disabled}
    >
      {children}
    </button>
  );
};

export default Button;

===== D:\projects\teacherTest\teacher-simulator-unified\client\src\components\ChoiceCard\ChoiceCard.jsx =====
import React from 'react';
import './ChoiceCard.css';

const ChoiceCard = ({ choice, solution, onClick, config }) => {
  const getIconForMethod = (method) => {
    const icons = config?.icons?.solutionMethods || {};
    return icons[method] || icons.default || 'fas fa-hand-point-right';
  };

  const getRiskColor = (risk) => {
    if (!risk) return 'unknown';
    if (risk <= 3) return 'low';
    if (risk <= 6) return 'medium';
    return 'high';
  };

  const getStrategyLabel = (strategy) => {
    const strategies = {
      'РЎРѕРїРµСЂРЅРёС‡РµСЃС‚РІРѕ': { icon: 'fas fa-fist-raised', color: 'strategy-competitive' },
      'РЎРѕС‚СЂСѓРґРЅРёС‡РµСЃС‚РІРѕ': { icon: 'fas fa-handshake', color: 'strategy-cooperative' },
      'РљРѕРјРїСЂРѕРјРёСЃСЃ': { icon: 'fas fa-balance-scale', color: 'strategy-compromise' },
      'РР·Р±РµРіР°РЅРёРµ': { icon: 'fas fa-running', color: 'strategy-avoidance' },
      'РџСЂРёСЃРїРѕСЃРѕР±Р»РµРЅРёРµ': { icon: 'fas fa-adjust', color: 'strategy-accommodation' },
      'РћР±СѓС‡РµРЅРёРµ': { icon: 'fas fa-graduation-cap', color: 'strategy-learning' },
      'РљРѕРЅС‚СЂРѕР»СЊ': { icon: 'fas fa-cogs', color: 'strategy-control' }
    };

    return strategies[strategy] || { icon: 'fas fa-question', color: 'strategy-default' };
  };

  const strategyInfo = getStrategyLabel(solution?.strategy);

  return (
    <button className="choice-card" onClick={onClick}>
      <div className="choice-header">
        <div className="choice-icon">
          <i className={getIconForMethod(solution?.method)}></i>
        </div>
        <h4>{choice.description}</h4>
      </div>

      {solution && (
        <div className="choice-details">


          {solution.description && (
            <p className="solution-description">
              <i className="fas fa-info-circle"></i> {solution.description}
            </p>
          )}

          <div className="solution-meta">
            <div className="solution-method">
              <span className="meta-label"><i className="fas fa-cogs"></i> РњРµС‚РѕРґ:</span>
              <span className="meta-value">{solution.method || 'РќРµ СѓРєР°Р·Р°РЅ'}</span>
            </div>

            <div className={`solution-strategy ${strategyInfo.color}`}>
              <span className="meta-label"><i className={strategyInfo.icon}></i> РЎС‚СЂР°С‚РµРіРёСЏ:</span>
              <span className="meta-value">{solution.strategy || 'РќРµ СѓРєР°Р·Р°РЅР°'}</span>
            </div>

            <div className={`solution-risk risk-${getRiskColor(solution.risk)}`}>
              <span className="meta-label"><i className="fas fa-exclamation-triangle"></i> Р РёСЃРє:</span>
              <span className="meta-value">
                {solution.risk !== undefined ? `${solution.risk}/10` : 'РќРµ СѓРєР°Р·Р°РЅ'}
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="solution-autonomy">
              <span className="meta-label"><i className="fas fa-user-check"></i> РђРІС‚РѕРЅРѕРјРёСЏ:</span>
              <span className="meta-value">
                {solution.autonomy !== undefined ? `${solution.autonomy}/10` : 'РќРµ СѓРєР°Р·Р°РЅР°'}
              </span>
            </div>

            {solution.duration && (
              <div className="solution-duration">
                <i className="fas fa-clock"></i>
                <span>{solution.duration}</span>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {solution.type && (
            <div className="solution-type">
              <span className="type-label">РўРёРї СЂРµС€РµРЅРёСЏ:</span>
              <span className="type-value">
                {solution.type === 'РўР°РєС‚РёС‡РµСЃРєРѕРµР РµС€РµРЅРёРµ' ? 'РўР°РєС‚РёС‡РµСЃРєРѕРµ' : 'РЎС‚СЂР°С‚РµРіРёС‡РµСЃРєРѕРµ'}
              </span>
            </div>
          )}


          <div class="conteiner_card">
          {solution.principles && solution.principles.length > 0 && (
            <div className="solution-principles">
              <span className="principles-label">
                <i className="fas fa-balance-scale"></i> РџРµРґР°РіРѕРіРёС‡РµСЃРєРёРµ РїСЂРёРЅС†РёРїС‹:
              </span>
              <div className="principles-tags">
                {solution.principles.map((principle, idx) => (
                  <span key={idx} className="principle-tag">{principle}</span>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {solution.resources && solution.resources.length > 0 && (
            <div className="solution-resources">
              <span className="resources-label">
                <i className="fas fa-toolbox"></i> Р РµСЃСѓСЂСЃС‹:
              </span>
              <div className="resources-tags">
                {solution.resources.map((resource, idx) => (
                  <span key={idx} className="resource-tag">{resource}</span>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {solution.strengthens && solution.strengthens.length > 0 && (
            <div className="solution-effects">
              <div className="strengthens">
                <span className="effects-label positive">
                  <i className="fas fa-plus-circle"></i> РЈРєСЂРµРїР»СЏРµС‚:
                </span>
                <div className="effects-tags">
                  {solution.strengthens.map((effect, idx) => (
                    <span key={idx} className="effect-tag positive">{effect}</span>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>

              {solution.weakens && solution.weakens.length > 0 && (
                <div className="weakens">
                  <span className="effects-label negative">
                    <i className="fas fa-minus-circle"></i> РћСЃР»Р°Р±Р»СЏРµС‚:
                  </span>
                  <div className="effects-tags">
                    {solution.weakens.map((effect, idx) => (
                      <span key={idx} className="effect-tag negative">{effect}</span>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="choice-footer">
        <span className="choose-text">
          <i className="fas fa-mouse-pointer"></i> Р’С‹Р±СЂР°С‚СЊ СЌС‚РѕС‚ РІР°СЂРёР°РЅС‚
        </span>
        <i className="fas fa-chevron-right"></i>
      </div>
    </button>
  );
};

export default ChoiceCard;

import React from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import PropTypes from 'prop-types';

// РРјРїРѕСЂС‚РёСЂСѓРµРј РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ РїСЂР°РІРёР»СЊРЅРѕ (РѕС‚РЅРѕСЃРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕ СЂР°СЃРїРѕР»РѕР¶РµРЅРёСЏ СЌС‚РѕРіРѕ С„Р°Р№Р»Р°)
import situationsData from '../../data/situations';

const DebugLink = ({ to, children, ...props }) => {
  const handleClick = (e) => {
    console.log('=== DEBUG NAVIGATION ===');
    console.log('Navigation to:', to);
    console.log('Available situation keys:', Object.keys(situationsData));

    // РР·РІР»РµРєР°РµРј sitId РёР· URL
    const urlParts = to.split('/');
    const sitId = urlParts[urlParts.length - 1];
    console.log('Extracted sitId:', sitId);

    // РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЏРµРј, СЃСѓС‰РµСЃС‚РІСѓРµС‚ Р»Рё СЃРёС‚СѓР°С†РёСЏ
    if (situationsData[sitId]) {
      console.log('вњ… РЎРёС‚СѓР°С†РёСЏ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅР°:', situationsData[sitId].title);
    } else {
      console.log('вќЊ РЎРёС‚СѓР°С†РёСЏ РќР• РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅР°!');
      console.log('РџРѕРїСЂРѕР±СѓР№С‚Рµ РѕРґРёРЅ РёР· СЌС‚РёС… ID:', Object.keys(situationsData));
    }
    console.log('=== END DEBUG ===');
  };

  return (
    <Link to={to} onClick={handleClick} {...props}>
      {children}
    </Link>
  );
};

DebugLink.propTypes = {
  to: PropTypes.string.isRequired,
  children: PropTypes.node.isRequired
};

export default DebugLink;

      classClimateChange: 'РР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёРµ РєР»РёРјР°С‚Р°',
      teacherAuthorityChange: 'РР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёРµ Р°РІС‚РѕСЂРёС‚РµС‚Р°',
      burnoutChange: 'РР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёРµ РІС‹РіРѕСЂР°РЅРёСЏ'
    };

    return translations[key] || key;
  };

  // Р¤СѓРЅРєС†РёСЏ РґР»СЏ С„РѕСЂРјР°С‚РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёСЏ Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёСЏ РјРµС‚СЂРёРєРё
  const formatMetricValue = (value) => {
    if (typeof value === 'number') {
      const formatted = value > 0 ? `+${value.toFixed(1)}` : value.toFixed(1);
      return formatted;
    }
    return value;
  };

  // Р¤СѓРЅРєС†РёРё РґР»СЏ СЂРµРЅРґРµСЂРёРЅРіР° СЂР°Р·РЅС‹С… С‚РёРїРѕРІ РґР°РЅРЅС‹С…
  const renderContext = () => (
    <>
      <div className="card-header">
        <div className="card-icon">
          <i className="fas fa-chalkboard"></i>
        </div>
        <div className="card-title">
          <h5>{data.subject} - {data.grade} РєР»Р°СЃСЃ</h5>
          <div className="card-subtitle">{data.lessonPhase}</div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="card-body">
        <p className="card-description">{data.description}</p>
        <div className="card-details">
          <div className="detail-item">
            <span className="detail-label">Р¤РѕСЂРјР°С‚:</span>
            <span className="detail-value">{data.format}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="detail-item">
            <span className="detail-label">Р”Р»РёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ:</span>
            <span className="detail-value">{data.duration}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="detail-item">
            <span className="detail-label">РћР±РѕСЂСѓРґРѕРІР°РЅРёРµ:</span>
            <span className="detail-value">{data.techEquipment}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );

  const renderParticipant = () => (
    <>
      <div className="card-header">
        <div className="card-icon">
          <i className={data.type === 'РЈС‡РёС‚РµР»СЊ' ? 'fas fa-chalkboard-teacher' : 'fas fa-user-graduate'}></i>
        </div>
        <div className="card-title">
          <h5>{data.name}</h5>
          <div className="card-subtitle">{data.type}</div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="card-body">
        {data.type === 'РЈС‡РёС‚РµР»СЊ' ? (
          <div className="card-details">
            <div className="detail-item">
              <span className="detail-label">РћРїС‹С‚:</span>
              <span className="detail-value">{data.experience} Р»РµС‚</span>
            </div>
            <div className="detail-item">
              <span className="detail-label">РЎС‚РёР»СЊ:</span>
              <span className="detail-value">{data.style}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="detail-item">
              <span className="detail-label">Р РѕР»СЊ:</span>
              <span className="detail-value">{data.role}</span>
            </div>
            {data.ei && (
              <div className="detail-item">
                <span className="detail-label">EI:</span>
                <span className="detail-value">{data.ei}</span>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="card-details">
            <div className="detail-item">
              <span className="detail-label">Р’РѕР·СЂР°СЃС‚:</span>
              <span className="detail-value">{data.age}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="detail-item">
              <span className="detail-label">РњРѕС‚РёРІР°С†РёСЏ:</span>
              <span className="detail-value">
                <div className="progress-bar">
                  <div
                    className="progress-fill"
                    style={{ width: `${data.motivation * 100}%` }}
                  ></div>
                  <span className="progress-text">{Math.round(data.motivation * 100)}%</span>
                </div>
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="detail-item">
              <span className="detail-label">РџРѕРґРіРѕС‚РѕРІРєР°:</span>
              <span className="detail-value">{data.preparation}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="detail-item">
              <span className="detail-label">РЎС‚РёР»СЊ РѕР±СѓС‡РµРЅРёСЏ:</span>
              <span className="detail-value">{data.style}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </>
  );

  const renderProblem = () => (
    <>
      <div className="card-header">
        <div className="card-icon">
          <i className="fas fa-exclamation-triangle"></i>
        </div>
        <div className="card-title">
          <h5>{data.type}</h5>
          <div className={`intensity-badge intensity-${data.intensity?.toLowerCase()}`}>
            {data.intensity}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="card-body">
        <p className="card-description">{data.description}</p>
        <div className="card-details">
          <div className="detail-item">
            <span className="detail-label">РСЃС‚РѕС‡РЅРёРє:</span>
            <span className="detail-value">{data.source}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="detail-item">
            <span className="detail-label">РќР°РїСЂСЏР¶РµРЅРЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ:</span>
            <span className="detail-value">
              <div className="tension-indicator">
                <div
                  className="tension-bar"
                  style={{ width: `${data.tensionFactor * 100}%` }}
                ></div>
                <span className="tension-value">{Math.round(data.tensionFactor * 100)}%</span>
              </div>
            </span>
          </div>
          {data.emotions && data.emotions.length > 0 && (
            <div className="detail-item">
              <span className="detail-label">Р­РјРѕС†РёРё:</span>
              <div className="emotions-tags">
                {data.emotions.slice(0, 3).map((emotion, idx) => (
                  <span key={idx} className="emotion-tag">{emotion}</span>
                ))}
                {data.emotions.length > 3 && (
                  <span className="emotion-tag">+{data.emotions.length - 3}</span>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );

  const renderSolution = () => (
    <>
      <div className="card-header">
        <div className="card-icon">
          <i className="fas fa-lightbulb"></i>
        </div>
        <div className="card-title">
          <h5>{data.method}</h5>
          <div className={`solution-type ${data.type?.replace('Р РµС€РµРЅРёРµ', '').toLowerCase()}`}>
            {data.type}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="card-body">
        <p className="card-description">{data.description}</p>
        <div className="card-details">
          <div className="detail-item">
            <span className="detail-label">РЎС‚СЂР°С‚РµРіРёСЏ:</span>
            <span className="detail-value">{data.strategy}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="detail-item">
            <span className="detail-label">Р РёСЃРє:</span>
            <span className="detail-value">
              <div className="risk-indicator">
                <div className={`risk-dot risk-${data.risk <= 3 ? 'low' : data.risk <= 6 ? 'medium' : 'high'}`}></div>
                <span>{data.risk}/10</span>
              </div>
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="detail-item">
            <span className="detail-label">РђРІС‚РѕРЅРѕРјРёСЏ:</span>
            <span className="detail-value">
              <div className="autonomy-indicator">
                <div
                  className="autonomy-bar"
                  style={{ width: `${data.autonomy * 10}%` }}
                ></div>
                <span>{data.autonomy}/10</span>
              </div>
            </span>
          </div>
          {data.duration && (
            <div className="detail-item">
              <span className="detail-label">Р”Р»РёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ:</span>
              <span className="detail-value">{data.duration}</span>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );

  const renderOutcome = () => (
    <>
      <div className="card-header">
        <div className="card-icon">
          <i className={`fas fa-${data.type === 'success' ? 'check-circle' : data.type === 'failure' ? 'times-circle' : 'minus-circle'}`}></i>
        </div>
        <div className="card-title">
          <h5>
            {data.type === 'success' ? 'РЈСЃРїРµС…' :
             data.type === 'failure' ? 'РџСЂРѕРІР°Р»' : 'Р§Р°СЃС‚РёС‡РЅС‹Р№ СѓСЃРїРµС…'}
          </h5>
          <div className={`outcome-intensity intensity-${data.intensity <= 33 ? 'low' : data.intensity <= 66 ? 'medium' : 'high'}`}>
            {data.intensity}/100
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="card-body">
        <p className="card-description">{data.text}</p>
        <div className="card-details">
          <div className="detail-item">
            <span className="detail-label">Р­С„С„РµРєС‚:</span>
            <span className="detail-value">{data.longTerm ? 'Р”РѕР»РіРѕСЃСЂРѕС‡РЅС‹Р№' : 'РљСЂР°С‚РєРѕСЃСЂРѕС‡РЅС‹Р№'}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="detail-item">
            <span className="detail-label">РћР±СЂР°С‚РёРјРѕСЃС‚СЊ:</span>
            <span className="detail-value">{data.reversible ? 'РћР±СЂР°С‚РёРјС‹Р№' : 'РќРµРѕР±СЂР°С‚РёРјС‹Р№'}</span>
          </div>
          {data.metrics && (
            <div className="detail-item full-width">
              <span className="detail-label">РњРµС‚СЂРёРєРё:</span>
              <div className="metrics-grid">
                {Object.entries(data.metrics).map(([key, value]) => (
                  <div key={key} className="metric-item">
                    <span className="metric-label">{translateMetricKey(key)}</span>
                    <span className={`metric-value ${value > 0 ? 'positive' : value < 0 ? 'negative' : 'neutral'}`}>
                      {formatMetricValue(value)}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );

  // Р’С‹Р±РѕСЂ СЂРµРЅРґРµСЂР° РїРѕ РєР°С‚РµРіРѕСЂРёРё
  const renderContent = () => {
    switch (category) {
      case 'contexts': return renderContext();
      case 'participants': return renderParticipant();
      case 'problems': return renderProblem();
      case 'solutions': return renderSolution();
      case 'outcomes': return renderOutcome();
      default: return <p>РќРµРёР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅР°СЏ РєР°С‚РµРіРѕСЂРёСЏ</p>;
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="library-data-card">
      {renderContent()}
      <div className="card-footer">
        <div className="card-id">
          <i className="fas fa-hashtag"></i>
          <span>{data.id}</span>
        </div>
        <button className="card-action">
          <i className="fas fa-edit"></i>
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default LibraryDataCard;

===== D:\projects\teacherTest\teacher-simulator-unified\client\src\components\OutcomeDisplay\OutcomeDisplay.jsx =====
import React from 'react';
import './OutcomeDisplay.css';

const OutcomeDisplay = ({ outcome, onRestart, onBackToStart, config }) => {
  if (!outcome) return null;

  // Р¤СѓРЅРєС†РёСЏ РґР»СЏ РїРµСЂРµРІРѕРґР° РєР»СЋС‡РµР№ РјРµС‚СЂРёРє РЅР° СЂСѓСЃСЃРєРёР№
  const translateMetricKey = (key) => {
    const translations = {
      motivation: 'РњРѕС‚РёРІР°С†РёСЏ',
      stress: 'РЎС‚СЂРµСЃСЃ',
      trust: 'Р”РѕРІРµСЂРёРµ',
      classClimate: 'РљР»РёРјР°С‚ РІ РєР»Р°СЃСЃРµ',
      teacherAuthority: 'РђРІС‚РѕСЂРёС‚РµС‚ СѓС‡РёС‚РµР»СЏ',
      burnout: 'Р’С‹РіРѕСЂР°РЅРёРµ',
      motivationChange: 'РР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёРµ РјРѕС‚РёРІР°С†РёРё',
      stressChange: 'РР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёРµ СЃС‚СЂРµСЃСЃР°',
      trustChange: 'РР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёРµ РґРѕРІРµСЂРёСЏ',
      classClimateChange: 'РР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёРµ РєР»РёРјР°С‚Р°',
      teacherAuthorityChange: 'РР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёРµ Р°РІС‚РѕСЂРёС‚РµС‚Р°',
      burnoutChange: 'РР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёРµ РІС‹РіРѕСЂР°РЅРёСЏ'
    };

    // РЈРґР°Р»СЏРµРј 'Change' РёР· РєР»СЋС‡Р° РґР»СЏ Р±Р°Р·РѕРІРѕРіРѕ РїРµСЂРµРІРѕРґР°, РµСЃР»Рё РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅРѕ РїРѕР»РЅРѕРµ СЃРѕРІРїР°РґРµРЅРёРµ
    const baseKey = key.replace('Change', '');
    return translations[key] || translations[baseKey] || key;
  };

  const getOutcomeTypeText = (type) => {
    const texts = config?.texts?.outcomeTypes || {};
    return texts[type] || 'Р РµР·СѓР»СЊС‚Р°С‚';
  };

  const getOutcomeIcon = (type) => {
    switch (type) {
      case 'success': return 'fas fa-check-circle';
      case 'partial': return 'fas fa-exclamation-circle';
      case 'failure': return 'fas fa-times-circle';
      default: return 'fas fa-info-circle';
    }
  };

  const formatMetrics = (metrics) => {
    if (!metrics) return [];

    return Object.entries(metrics).map(([key, value]) => {
      // РџРµСЂРµРІРѕРґРёРј РєР»СЋС‡ РјРµС‚СЂРёРєРё РЅР° СЂСѓСЃСЃРєРёР№
      const translatedKey = translateMetricKey(key);
      const sign = value >= 0 ? '+' : '';
      const valueClass = value > 0 ? 'positive' : value < 0 ? 'negative' : 'neutral';

      return {
        key: translatedKey,
        originalKey: key,
        value,
        sign,
        valueClass,
        absoluteValue: Math.abs(value)
      };
    });
  };

  const metrics = formatMetrics(outcome.metrics);
  const outcomeTypeText = getOutcomeTypeText(outcome.type);
  const outcomeIcon = getOutcomeIcon(outcome.type);

  return (
    <div className={`outcome-card outcome-${outcome.type}`}>
      <div className="outcome-header">
        <div className="outcome-title">
          <i className={`outcome-icon ${outcomeIcon}`}></i>
          <h3>{outcomeTypeText}</h3>
        </div>

        <div className="outcome-intensity">
          <div className="intensity-label">РРЅС‚РµРЅСЃРёРІРЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ РІРѕР·РґРµР№СЃС‚РІРёСЏ:</div>
          <div className="intensity-value">
            <div className="intensity-bar">
              <div
                className="intensity-fill"
                style={{ width: `${outcome.intensity || 0}%` }}
              ></div>
            </div>
            <strong>{outcome.intensity || 0}/100</strong>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="outcome-content">
        <div className="outcome-text-container">
          <i className="fas fa-quote-left"></i>
          <p className="outcome-text">{outcome.text}</p>
          <i className="fas fa-quote-right"></i>
        </div>

        <div className="outcome-meta">
          <div className="meta-grid">
            <div className="meta-item">
              <div className="meta-icon">
                <i className="fas fa-clock"></i>
              </div>
              <div className="meta-content">
                <div className="meta-label">Р”Р»РёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ СЌС„С„РµРєС‚Р°</div>
                <div className="meta-value">
                  {outcome.longTerm ? 'Р”РѕР»РіРѕСЃСЂРѕС‡РЅС‹Р№' : 'РљСЂР°С‚РєРѕСЃСЂРѕС‡РЅС‹Р№'}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="meta-item">
              <div className="meta-icon">
                <i className="fas fa-undo"></i>
              </div>
              <div className="meta-content">
                <div className="meta-label">РћР±СЂР°С‚РёРјРѕСЃС‚СЊ</div>
                <div className="meta-value">
                  {outcome.reversible ? 'РћР±СЂР°С‚РёРјС‹Р№' : 'РќРµРѕР±СЂР°С‚РёРјС‹Р№'}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {metrics.length > 0 && (
          <div className="outcome-metrics">
            <h4><i className="fas fa-chart-line"></i> РР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёСЏ РјРµС‚СЂРёРє:</h4>
            <div className="metrics-grid">
              {metrics.map((item, index) => (
                <div key={index} className="metric-item">
                  <div className="metric-info">
                    <div className="metric-label">{item.key}</div>
                    <div className={`metric-value ${item.valueClass}`}>
                      {item.sign}{item.value.toFixed(1)}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="metric-bar-container">
                    <div
                      className={`metric-bar ${item.valueClass}`}
                      style={{
                        width: `${Math.min(item.absoluteValue * 100, 100)}%`,
                        transform: item.value < 0 ? 'scaleX(-1)' : 'none'
                      }}
                    ></div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>

            <div className="metrics-summary">
              <div className="summary-item">
                <i className="fas fa-arrow-up positive"></i>
                <span>РџРѕР»РѕР¶РёС‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РёР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёСЏ: {
                  metrics.filter(m => m.value > 0).length
                }</span>
              </div>
              <div className="summary-item">
                <i className="fas fa-arrow-down negative"></i>
                <span>РћС‚СЂРёС†Р°С‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РёР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёСЏ: {
                  metrics.filter(m => m.value < 0).length
                }</span>
              </div>
              <div className="summary-item">
                <i className="fas fa-equals neutral"></i>
                <span>РќРµР№С‚СЂР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РёР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёСЏ: {
                  metrics.filter(m => m.value === 0).length
                }</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="outcome-actions">
          <button className="btn btn-primary" onClick={onRestart}>
            <i className="fas fa-redo"></i> РќР°С‡Р°С‚СЊ Р·Р°РЅРѕРІРѕ
          </button>
          <button className="btn btn-outline" onClick={onBackToStart}>
            <i className="fas fa-arrow-left"></i> Рљ РЅР°С‡Р°Р»Сѓ СЃРёС‚СѓР°С†РёРё
          </button>
          <button className="btn btn-secondary" onClick={() => window.location.href = '/catalog'}>
            <i className="fas fa-list"></i> Р’ РєР°С‚Р°Р»РѕРі
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default OutcomeDisplay;

// src/components/ScenarioCard/ScenarioCard.jsx
import React from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import PropTypes from 'prop-types';
import './ScenarioCard.css';

// РљРѕРЅС„РёРіСѓСЂР°С†РёСЏ РёРєРѕРЅРѕРє
const ICON_MAP = {
  'lightbulb': 'fas fa-lightbulb',
  'tasks': 'fas fa-tasks',
  'comments': 'fas fa-comments',
  'user-injured': 'fas fa-user-injured',
  'user-friends': 'fas fa-user-friends',
  'user-shield': 'fas fa-user-shield',
  'users': 'fas fa-users',
  'shield-alt': 'fas fa-shield-alt',
  'chalkboard-teacher': 'fas fa-chalkboard-teacher',
  'brain': 'fas fa-brain',
  'gavel': 'fas fa-gavel',
  'scale-balanced': 'fas fa-scale-balanced',
  'users-slash': 'fas fa-users-slash',
  'book': 'fas fa-book',
  'laptop-code': 'fas fa-laptop-code',
  'flask': 'fas fa-flask',
  'question-circle': 'fas fa-question-circle'
};

const DIFFICULTY_CONFIG = {
  easy: { class: 'difficulty-easy', text: 'Р‘Р°Р·РѕРІС‹Р№' },
  medium: { class: 'difficulty-medium', text: 'РЎСЂРµРґРЅРёР№' },
  hard: { class: 'difficulty-hard', text: 'РџСЂРѕРґРІРёРЅСѓС‚С‹Р№' },
  Р±Р°Р·РѕРІС‹Р№: { class: 'difficulty-easy', text: 'Р‘Р°Р·РѕРІС‹Р№' },
  СЃСЂРµРґРЅРёР№: { class: 'difficulty-medium', text: 'РЎСЂРµРґРЅРёР№' },
  РїСЂРѕРґРІРёРЅСѓС‚С‹Р№: { class: 'difficulty-hard', text: 'РџСЂРѕРґРІРёРЅСѓС‚С‹Р№' }
};

const ScenarioCard = ({
  title,
  description,
  difficulty = 'medium',
  type,
  duration = '15',
  typeIcon = 'question-circle',
  id,
  dataType = 'scenario'
}) => {
  // РџСЂРµРѕР±СЂР°Р·СѓРµРј id РІ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєСѓ
  const scenarioId = String(id);

  // РџРѕР»СѓС‡Р°РµРј РЅР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєРё СЃР»РѕР¶РЅРѕСЃС‚Рё
  const normalizedDifficulty = difficulty.toString().toLowerCase();
  const difficultyConfig = DIFFICULTY_CONFIG[normalizedDifficulty] || DIFFICULTY_CONFIG.medium;

  // РџРѕР»СѓС‡Р°РµРј РєР»Р°СЃСЃ РёРєРѕРЅРєРё
  const getIconClass = () => {
    if (typeIcon?.startsWith('fas ') || typeIcon?.startsWith('far ')) {
      return typeIcon;
    }
    return ICON_MAP[typeIcon] || 'fas fa-question-circle';
  };

  // РћР±СЂРµР·Р°РµРј РѕРїРёСЃР°РЅРёРµ
  const truncateDescription = (text, maxLength = 120) => {
    if (!text) return '';
    return text.length <= maxLength ? text : `${text.substring(0, maxLength)}...`;
  };

  // РћР±СЂР°Р±РѕС‚С‡РёРє РєР»РёРєР° РґР»СЏ РѕС‚Р»Р°РґРєРё
  const handleClick = () => {
    console.log('=== ScenarioCard Debug ===');
    console.log('Scenario ID:', scenarioId);
    console.log('Title:', title);
    console.log('Navigation to:', `/situation/${scenarioId}`);
  };

  return (
    <div
      className={`scenario-card ${difficultyConfig.class}`}
      data-type={dataType}
      data-difficulty={normalizedDifficulty}
      data-id={scenarioId}
    >
      {/* Р‘РµР№РґР¶ СЃР»РѕР¶РЅРѕСЃС‚Рё */}
      <div className="scenario-badge">
        <span className={`badge ${difficultyConfig.class}`}>
          {difficultyConfig.text}
        </span>
      </div>

      {/* Р—Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРѕРє */}
      <h3 className="scenario-title" title={title}>
        {title}
      </h3>

      {/* РћРїРёСЃР°РЅРёРµ */}
      <p className="scenario-description" title={description}>
        {truncateDescription(description)}
      </p>

      {/* РњРµС‚Р°-РёРЅС„РѕСЂРјР°С†РёСЏ */}
      <div className="scenario-meta">
        <div className="meta-item" title={`РџСЂРѕРґРѕР»Р¶РёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ: ${duration} РјРёРЅСѓС‚`}>
          <i className="far fa-clock"></i>
          <span>~{duration} РјРёРЅ</span>
        </div>

        <div className="meta-item" title={`РўРёРї: ${type}`}>
          <i className={getIconClass()}></i>
          <span>{type}</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* РљРЅРѕРїРєР° РїРµСЂРµС…РѕРґР° */}
      <div className="scenario-actions">
        <Link
          to={`/situation/${scenarioId}`}
          className="btn btn-primary btn-sm"
          onClick={handleClick}
          aria-label={`РќР°С‡Р°С‚СЊ СЃРёС‚СѓР°С†РёСЋ "${title}"`}
        >
          <i className="fas fa-play"></i>
          <span>РќР°С‡Р°С‚СЊ</span>
        </Link>
      </div>

      {/* Р”РѕРїРѕР»РЅРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹Рµ С‚РµРіРё */}
      <div className="scenario-tags">
        <span className="tag" title="РўРёРї СЃРёС‚СѓР°С†РёРё">{type}</span>
        <span className="tag" title="РЎР»РѕР¶РЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ">{difficultyConfig.text}</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

// PropTypes РґР»СЏ РІР°Р»РёРґР°С†РёРё РїСЂРѕРїСЃРѕРІ
ScenarioCard.propTypes = {
  title: PropTypes.string.isRequired,
  description: PropTypes.string.isRequired,
  difficulty: PropTypes.oneOfType([
    PropTypes.string,
    PropTypes.number
  ]),
  type: PropTypes.string.isRequired,
  duration: PropTypes.oneOfType([
    PropTypes.string,
    PropTypes.number
  ]),
  typeIcon: PropTypes.string,
  id: PropTypes.oneOfType([
    PropTypes.string,
    PropTypes.number
  ]).isRequired,
  dataType: PropTypes.string
};

// Р”РѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚Р°С†РёСЏ РєРѕРјРїРѕРЅРµРЅС‚Р° РґР»СЏ СЂР°Р·СЂР°Р±РѕС‚С‡РёРєРѕРІ
ScenarioCard.displayName = 'ScenarioCard';
ScenarioCard.__docgenInfo = {

  props: {

    },
    id: {
      type: 'string|number',
      required: true,
      description: 'РЈРЅРёРєР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ РёРґРµРЅС‚РёС„РёРєР°С‚РѕСЂ СЃС†РµРЅР°СЂРёСЏ (РґРѕР»Р¶РµРЅ СЃРѕРІРїР°РґР°С‚СЊ СЃ РєР»СЋС‡РѕРј РІ situations.js)'
    },

    }

import React from 'react';
import './SimpleSpiderChart.css';

const SimpleSpiderChart = ({ metrics, title = "Р”РёР°РіСЂР°РјРјР° РёР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёР№ РјРµС‚СЂРёРє" }) => {
  if (!metrics || Object.keys(metrics).length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="simple-spider-chart">
        <h4><i className="fas fa-chart-radar"></i> {title}</h4>
        <div className="no-metrics">
          <i className="fas fa-chart-line"></i>
          <p>РќРµС‚ РґР°РЅРЅС‹С… РјРµС‚СЂРёРє РґР»СЏ РѕС‚РѕР±СЂР°Р¶РµРЅРёСЏ</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // Р¤СѓРЅРєС†РёСЏ РґР»СЏ РїРµСЂРµРІРѕРґР° РєР»СЋС‡РµР№ РјРµС‚СЂРёРє РЅР° СЂСѓСЃСЃРєРёР№
  const translateMetricKey = (key, short = false) => {
    const translations = {
      motivation: short ? 'РњРѕС‚РёРІР°С†РёСЏ' : 'РњРѕС‚РёРІР°С†РёСЏ',
      stress: short ? 'РЎС‚СЂРµСЃСЃ' : 'РЎС‚СЂРµСЃСЃ',
      trust: short ? 'Р”РѕРІРµСЂРёРµ' : 'Р”РѕРІРµСЂРёРµ',
      classClimate: short ? 'РљР»РёРјР°С‚' : 'РљР»РёРјР°С‚ РІ РєР»Р°СЃСЃРµ',
      teacherAuthority: short ? 'РђРІС‚РѕСЂРёС‚РµС‚' : 'РђРІС‚РѕСЂРёС‚РµС‚ СѓС‡РёС‚РµР»СЏ',
      burnout: short ? 'Р’С‹РіРѕСЂР°РЅРёРµ' : 'Р’С‹РіРѕСЂР°РЅРёРµ',
      motivationChange: short ? 'РњРѕС‚РёРІР°С†РёСЏ' : 'РР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёРµ РјРѕС‚РёРІР°С†РёРё',
      stressChange: short ? 'РЎС‚СЂРµСЃСЃ' : 'РР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёРµ СЃС‚СЂРµСЃСЃР°',
      trustChange: short ? 'Р”РѕРІРµСЂРёРµ' : 'РР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёРµ РґРѕРІРµСЂРёСЏ',
      classClimateChange: short ? 'РљР»РёРјР°С‚' : 'РР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёРµ РєР»РёРјР°С‚Р°',
      teacherAuthorityChange: short ? 'РђРІС‚РѕСЂРёС‚РµС‚' : 'РР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёРµ Р°РІС‚РѕСЂРёС‚РµС‚Р°',
      burnoutChange: short ? 'Р’С‹РіРѕСЂР°РЅРёРµ' : 'РР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёРµ РІС‹РіРѕСЂР°РЅРёСЏ'
    };

    const baseKey = key.replace('Change', '');
    const result = translations[key] || translations[baseKey] || key;

    // РЎРѕРєСЂР°С‰Р°РµРј СЃР»РёС€РєРѕРј РґР»РёРЅРЅС‹Рµ РЅР°Р·РІР°РЅРёСЏ
    if (short && result.length > 8) {
      return result.substring(0, 8) + (result.length > 8 ? '' : '');
    }

    return result;
  };

  // РџРѕРґРіРѕС‚Р°РІР»РёРІР°РµРј РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ
  const metricsList = Object.entries(metrics).map(([key, value]) => {
    // РќРѕСЂРјР°Р»РёР·СѓРµРј Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёСЏ РѕС‚ -1 РґРѕ 1 РґРѕ 0-100
    const normalizedValue = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, (value + 1) * 50));

    return {
      name: translateMetricKey(key.replace('Change', ''), true), // РљРѕСЂРѕС‚РєРёРµ РІРµСЂСЃРёРё РґР»СЏ РґРёР°РіСЂР°РјРјС‹
      fullName: translateMetricKey(key.replace('Change', ''), false), // РџРѕР»РЅС‹Рµ РІРµСЂСЃРёРё РґР»СЏ Р»РµРіРµРЅРґС‹
      value: normalizedValue,
      actualValue: value,
      color: value > 0 ? '#4caf50' : value < 0 ? '#f44336' : '#9e9e9e'
    };
  });

  const numMetrics = metricsList.length;
  const angleStep = (2 * Math.PI) / numMetrics;

  return (
    <div className="simple-spider-chart">
      <h4><i className="fas fa-chart-radar"></i> {title}</h4>
      <div className="spider-container">
        <svg className="spider-svg" viewBox="0 0 250 250" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet">
          {/* Р’РЅРµС€РЅРёР№ РєСЂСѓРі */}
          <circle cx="125" cy="125" r="100" fill="none" stroke="#eee" strokeWidth="1" />

          {/* Р’РЅСѓС‚СЂРµРЅРЅРёРµ РєСЂСѓРіРё */}
          <circle cx="125" cy="125" r="75" fill="none" stroke="#eee" strokeWidth="1" />
          <circle cx="125" cy="125" r="50" fill="none" stroke="#eee" strokeWidth="1" />
          <circle cx="125" cy="125" r="25" fill="none" stroke="#eee" strokeWidth="1" />

          {/* Р›РёРЅРёРё РѕСЃРµР№ */}
          {metricsList.map((_, index) => {
            const angle = angleStep * index;
            const x2 = 125 + 100 * Math.cos(angle);
            const y2 = 125 + 100 * Math.sin(angle);
            return (
              <line
                key={`axis-${index}`}
                x1="125"
                y1="125"
                x2={x2}
                y2={y2}
                stroke="#ddd"
                strokeWidth="1"
              />
            );
          })}

          {/* РџРѕР»РёРіРѕРЅ СЃ РјРµС‚СЂРёРєР°РјРё */}
          <polygon
            points={metricsList.map((metric, index) => {
              const angle = angleStep * index;
              const r = metric.value; // РСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµРј РЅРѕСЂРјР°Р»РёР·РѕРІР°РЅРЅРѕРµ Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёРµ РЅР°РїСЂСЏРјСѓСЋ
              const x = 125 + r * Math.cos(angle);
              const y = 125 + r * Math.sin(angle);
              return `${x},${y}`;
            }).join(' ')}
            fill="rgba(136, 132, 216, 0.3)"
            stroke="#8884d8"
            strokeWidth="2"
          />

          {/* РўРѕС‡РєРё РјРµС‚СЂРёРє */}
          {metricsList.map((metric, index) => {
            const angle = angleStep * index;
            const r = metric.value;
            const x = 125 + r * Math.cos(angle);
            const y = 125 + r * Math.sin(angle);
            return (
              <circle
                key={`point-${index}`}
                cx={x}
                cy={y}
                r="5"
                fill="#8884d8"
                stroke="white"
                strokeWidth="2"
              />
            );
          })}

          {/* РџРѕРґРїРёСЃРё РјРµС‚СЂРёРє СЃ СѓР»СѓС‡С€РµРЅРЅС‹Рј РїРѕР·РёС†РёРѕРЅРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёРµРј */}
          {metricsList.map((metric, index) => {
            const angle = angleStep * index;
            const labelRadius = 112; // Р Р°РґРёСѓСЃ РґР»СЏ СЂР°Р·РјРµС‰РµРЅРёСЏ РїРѕРґРїРёСЃРµР№
            const x = 125 + labelRadius * Math.cos(angle);
            const y = 125 + labelRadius * Math.sin(angle);

            // РћРїСЂРµРґРµР»СЏРµРј РєРІР°РґСЂР°РЅС‚ РґР»СЏ РїСЂР°РІРёР»СЊРЅРѕРіРѕ РІС‹СЂР°РІРЅРёРІР°РЅРёСЏ
            const quadrant = Math.floor((angle * 2) / Math.PI) % 4;

            let textAnchor = "middle";
            let dy = "0.35em";

            // РќР°СЃС‚СЂР°РёРІР°РµРј РІС‹СЂР°РІРЅРёРІР°РЅРёРµ РІ Р·Р°РІРёСЃРёРјРѕСЃС‚Рё РѕС‚ РєРІР°РґСЂР°РЅС‚Р°
            switch (quadrant) {
              case 0: // РџСЂР°РІС‹Р№ РІРµСЂС…РЅРёР№ РєРІР°РґСЂР°РЅС‚
                textAnchor = "start";
                dy = "-0.5em";
                break;
              case 1: // Р›РµРІС‹Р№ РІРµСЂС…РЅРёР№ РєРІР°РґСЂР°РЅС‚
                textAnchor = "end";
                dy = "-0.5em";
                break;
              case 2: // Р›РµРІС‹Р№ РЅРёР¶РЅРёР№ РєРІР°РґСЂР°РЅС‚
                textAnchor = "end";
                dy = "1.2em";
                break;
              case 3: // РџСЂР°РІС‹Р№ РЅРёР¶РЅРёР№ РєРІР°РґСЂР°РЅС‚
                textAnchor = "start";
                dy = "1.2em";
                break;
            }

            // Р”Р»СЏ РІРµСЂС‚РёРєР°Р»СЊРЅС‹С… Рё РіРѕСЂРёР·РѕРЅС‚Р°Р»СЊРЅС‹С… РѕСЃРµР№
            if (Math.abs(Math.cos(angle)) < 0.1) { // Р’РµСЂС‚РёРєР°Р»СЊРЅРѕ
              textAnchor = "middle";
              dy = Math.sin(angle) > 0 ? "1.2em" : "-0.5em";
            } else if (Math.abs(Math.sin(angle)) < 0.1) { // Р“РѕСЂРёР·РѕРЅС‚Р°Р»СЊРЅРѕ
              textAnchor = Math.cos(angle) > 0 ? "start" : "end";
              dy = "0.35em";
            }

            return (
              <text
                key={`label-${index}`}
                x={x}
                y={y}
                textAnchor={textAnchor}
                dy={dy}
                fontSize="10"
                fill="#333"
                fontWeight="500"
                className="spider-label"
              >
                {metric.name}
              </text>
            );
          })}
        </svg>
      </div>

      {/* Р›РµРіРµРЅРґР° СЃ СЂРµР°Р»СЊРЅС‹РјРё Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёСЏРјРё */}
      <div className="spider-legend">
        {metricsList.map((metric, index) => (
          <div key={index} className="legend-row">
            <div className="legend-label">
              <span className="legend-dot" style={{ backgroundColor: metric.color }}></span>
              <span className="metric-name">{metric.fullName}</span>
            </div>
            <span className={`metric-value ${metric.actualValue > 0 ? 'positive' : metric.actualValue < 0 ? 'negative' : 'neutral'}`}>
              {metric.actualValue > 0 ? '+' : ''}{metric.actualValue.toFixed(1)}
            </span>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="chart-description">
        <p><i className="fas fa-info-circle"></i> Р—РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёСЏ РЅРѕСЂРјР°Р»РёР·РѕРІР°РЅС‹ РґР»СЏ РѕС‚РѕР±СЂР°Р¶РµРЅРёСЏ РѕС‚ 0 РґРѕ 100.</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default SimpleSpiderChart;

===== D:\projects\teacherTest\teacher-simulator-unified\client\src\components\SituationViewer\SituationViewer.jsx =====
import React from 'react';
import './SituationViewer.css';

const SituationViewer = ({
  title,
  description,
  image,
  context,
  problem,
  participants,
  difficulty,
  config
}) => {
  const defaultImage = config?.display?.defaultImage || 'https://via.placeholder.com/400x300/cccccc/333333?text=РР·РѕР±СЂР°Р¶РµРЅРёРµ+СЃРёС‚СѓР°С†РёРё';

  const getIconForProblem = (problemType) => {
    const icons = config?.icons?.problemTypes || {};
    return icons[problemType] || icons.default || 'fas fa-question';
  };

  const getDifficultyBadge = (diff) => {
    if (!diff) return null;

    let className = 'difficulty-badge';
    let text = diff;

    if (diff.includes('РЎСЂРµРґРЅ') || diff === 'РЎСЂРµРґРЅРёР№') {
      className += ' difficulty-medium';
      text = 'РЎСЂРµРґРЅРёР№';
    } else if (diff.includes('Р’С‹СЃРѕРє') || diff === 'Р’С‹СЃРѕРєРёР№') {
      className += ' difficulty-hard';
      text = 'РџСЂРѕРґРІРёРЅСѓС‚С‹Р№';
    } else {
      className += ' difficulty-easy';
      text = 'Р‘Р°Р·РѕРІС‹Р№';
    }

    return <span className={className}>{text}</span>;
  };

  return (
    <div className="situation-viewer">
      {/* Р—Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРѕРє Рё С‚РёРї РїСЂРѕР±Р»РµРјС‹ РІРІРµСЂС…Сѓ */}
      <div className="situation-header">
        <div className="header-main">
          <h1>{title}</h1>
          {problem && (
            <div className="problem-type-main">
              <i className={getIconForProblem(problem.type)}></i>
              <span className="problem-type-text">{problem.type}</span>
              {difficulty && getDifficultyBadge(difficulty)}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* РњРµС‚Р°-РёРЅС„РѕСЂРјР°С†РёСЏ */}
        <div className="situation-meta">
          {context && (
            <>
              <span className="meta-item">
                <i className="fas fa-clock"></i>
                <span className="meta-text">{context.duration || '~40 РјРёРЅ'}</span>
              </span>
              <span className="meta-item">
                <i className="fas fa-book"></i>
                <span className="meta-text">{context.subject}, {context.grade} РєР»Р°СЃСЃ</span>
              </span>
              <span className="meta-item">
                <i className="fas fa-tasks"></i>
                <span className="meta-text">{context.lessonPhase}</span>
              </span>
              <span className="meta-item">
                <i className="fas fa-laptop"></i>
                <span className="meta-text">{context.techEquipment}</span>
              </span>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Р”РµС‚Р°Р»СЊРЅР°СЏ РёРЅС„РѕСЂРјР°С†РёСЏ Рѕ РєРѕРЅС‚РµРєСЃС‚Рµ */}
      {context && (
        <div className="context-detail-section">
          <h4><i className="fas fa-info-circle"></i> РљРѕРЅС‚РµРєСЃС‚ СѓСЂРѕРєР°</h4>
          <div className="context-grid">
            <div className="context-item">
              <i className="fas fa-chalkboard"></i>
              <div>
                <div className="context-label">РџСЂРµРґРјРµС‚</div>
                <div className="context-value">{context.subject}</div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="context-item">
              <i className="fas fa-user-graduate"></i>
              <div>
                <div className="context-label">РљР»Р°СЃСЃ</div>
                <div className="context-value">{context.grade}</div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="context-item">
              <i className="fas fa-tasks"></i>
              <div>
                <div className="context-label">Р­С‚Р°Рї СѓСЂРѕРєР°</div>
                <div className="context-value">{context.lessonPhase}</div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="context-item">
              <i className="fas fa-laptop"></i>
              <div>
                <div className="context-label">РћР±РѕСЂСѓРґРѕРІР°РЅРёРµ</div>
                <div className="context-value">{context.techEquipment}</div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="context-item">
              <i className="fas fa-calendar-alt"></i>
              <div>
                <div className="context-label">Р¤РѕСЂРјР°С‚</div>
                <div className="context-value">{context.format}</div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="context-item">
              <i className="fas fa-hourglass-half"></i>
              <div>
                <div className="context-label">Р”Р»РёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ</div>
                <div className="context-value">{context.duration}</div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Р”РµС‚Р°Р»СЊРЅР°СЏ РёРЅС„РѕСЂРјР°С†РёСЏ Рѕ РїСЂРѕР±Р»РµРјРµ */}
      {problem && (
        <div className="problem-detail-section">
          <h4><i className="fas fa-exclamation-triangle"></i> РђРЅР°Р»РёР· РїСЂРѕР±Р»РµРјС‹</h4>
          <div className="problem-content">
            <div className="problem-description">
              <div className="problem-title">{problem.type}</div>
              <p>{problem.description}</p>
            </div>
            <div className="problem-metadata">
              <div className="metadata-item">
                <div className="metadata-label">
                  <i className="fas fa-signal"></i> РРЅС‚РµРЅСЃРёРІРЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ
                </div>
                <div className="metadata-value">{problem.intensity || 'РќРµ СѓРєР°Р·Р°РЅР°'}</div>
              </div>
              <div className="metadata-item">
                <div className="metadata-label">
                  <i className="fas fa-map-marker-alt"></i> РСЃС‚РѕС‡РЅРёРє
                </div>
                <div className="metadata-value">{problem.source || 'РќРµ СѓРєР°Р·Р°РЅ'}</div>
              </div>
              {problem.emotions && problem.emotions.length > 0 && (
                <div className="metadata-item">
                  <div className="metadata-label">
                    <i className="fas fa-smile"></i> Р­РјРѕС†РёРё
                  </div>
                  <div className="metadata-value">
                    <div className="emotions-tags">
                      {problem.emotions.map((emotion, index) => (
                        <span key={index} className="emotion-tag">{emotion}</span>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
              {problem.tensionFactor && (
                <div className="metadata-item">
                  <div className="metadata-label">
                    <i className="fas fa-chart-line"></i> РЈСЂРѕРІРµРЅСЊ РЅР°РїСЂСЏР¶РµРЅРЅРѕСЃС‚Рё
                  </div>
                  <div className="metadata-value">
                    <div className="tension-bar">
                      <div
                        className="tension-fill"
                        style={{ width: `${problem.tensionFactor * 100}%` }}
                      ></div>
                      <span className="tension-value">{Math.round(problem.tensionFactor * 100)}%</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* РЈС‡Р°СЃС‚РЅРёРєРё СЃРёС‚СѓР°С†РёРё */}
      {participants && participants.length > 0 && (
        <div className="participants-section">
          <h4><i className="fas fa-users"></i> РЈС‡Р°СЃС‚РЅРёРєРё СЃРёС‚СѓР°С†РёРё</h4>
          <div className="participants-grid">
            {participants.map((p, index) => (
              <div key={index} className="participant-card">
                <div className="participant-header">
                  <div className="participant-avatar">
                    {p.type === 'РЈС‡РёС‚РµР»СЊ' ? 'рџ‘ЁвЂЌрџЏ«' : 'рџ‘ЁвЂЌрџЋ“'}
                  </div>
                  <div className="participant-info">
                    <div className="participant-name">{p.name}</div>
                    <div className="participant-type">{p.type}</div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="participant-details">
                  {p.type === 'РЈС‡РёС‚РµР»СЊ' ? (
                    <>
                      <div className="detail-item">
                        <span className="detail-label">РћРїС‹С‚:</span>
                        <span className="detail-value">{p.experience} Р»РµС‚</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="detail-item">
                        <span className="detail-label">РЎС‚РёР»СЊ:</span>
                        <span className="detail-value">{p.style}</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="detail-item">
                        <span className="detail-label">Р РѕР»СЊ:</span>
                        <span className="detail-value">{p.role}</span>
                      </div>
                      {p.ei && (
                        <div className="detail-item">
                          <span className="detail-label">EI:</span>
                          <span className="detail-value">{p.ei}</span>
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <div className="detail-item">
                        <span className="detail-label">Р’РѕР·СЂР°СЃС‚:</span>
                        <span className="detail-value">{p.age}</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="detail-item">
                        <span className="detail-label">РџРѕРґРіРѕС‚РѕРІРєР°:</span>
                        <span className="detail-value">{p.preparation}</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="detail-item">
                        <span className="detail-label">РњРѕС‚РёРІР°С†РёСЏ:</span>
                        <span className="detail-value">
                          <div className="motivation-bar">
                            <div
                              className="motivation-fill"
                              style={{ width: `${p.motivation * 100}%` }}
                            ></div>
                            <span className="motivation-text">{Math.round(p.motivation * 100)}%</span>
                          </div>
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      {p.style && (
                        <div className="detail-item">
                          <span className="detail-label">РЎС‚РёР»СЊ РѕР±СѓС‡РµРЅРёСЏ:</span>
                          <span className="detail-value">{p.style}</span>
                        </div>
                      )}
                      {p.socialStatus && (
                        <div className="detail-item">
                          <span className="detail-label">РЎС‚Р°С‚СѓСЃ:</span>
                          <span className="detail-value">{p.socialStatus}</span>
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
          {/* Р‘Р»РѕРє СЃ РѕРїРёСЃР°РЅРёРµРј Рё РєР°СЂС‚РёРЅРєРѕР№ РІ СЂСЏРґ */}
      <div className="situation-description-block">
        <div className="description-content">
          <h3>РћРїРёСЃР°РЅРёРµ СЃРёС‚СѓР°С†РёРё</h3>
          <div className="description-text">{description}</div>
        </div>

        <div className="description-image">
          <img
            src={image || defaultImage}
            alt={title}
            onError={(e) => {
              e.target.src = defaultImage;
            }}
          />
        </div>
      </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default SituationViewer;

            text: "РЎРґРµР»Р°С‚СЊ РїСѓР±Р»РёС‡РЅРѕРµ Р·Р°РјРµС‡Р°РЅРёРµ",
            solutionRef: "sol_12",
            next: "outcome1"
          },
          {

            solutionRef: "sol_13",
            next: "node2"
          }

      },
      node2: {
        id: "node2",
        description: "РђРЅРґСЂРµР№ РіРѕРІРѕСЂРёС‚, С‡С‚Рѕ РјР°С‚РµСЂРёР°Р» РµРјСѓ РЅРµРёРЅС‚РµСЂРµСЃРµРЅ.",
        choices: [
          {
            id: "choice3",
            text: "РџСЂРµРґР»РѕР¶РёС‚СЊ РµРјСѓ РїРѕРјРѕС‰СЊ РїРѕСЃР»Рµ СѓСЂРѕРєР°",
            solutionRef: "sol_13",
            next: "outcome2"
          }
        ]
      },
      outcome1: {
        id: "outcome1",
        description: "РђРЅРґСЂРµР№ РѕР±РёРґРµР»СЃСЏ Рё РЅР°С‡Р°Р» РїСЂРѕС‚РµСЃС‚РѕРІР°С‚СЊ.",
        outcomeRef: "out_failure_16"
      },
      outcome2: {
        id: "outcome2",
        description: "РђРЅРґСЂРµР№ СЃРѕРіР»Р°СЃРёР»СЃСЏ РѕР±СЃСѓРґРёС‚СЊ РїСЂРѕР±Р»РµРјСѓ РїРѕСЃР»Рµ СѓСЂРѕРєР°.",
        outcomeRef: "out_success_1"
      }
    }
  }
};

export default situations;


===== D:\projects\teacherTest\teacher-simulator-unified\client\src\hooks\useSituation.js =====
import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import { useParams, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import situationsData from '../data/situations';
import libraryData from '../data/library';

export const useSituation = () => {
  const { sitId, nodeId } = useParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [situation, setSituation] = useState(null);
  const [currentNode, setCurrentNode] = useState(null);
  const [choiceHistory, setChoiceHistory] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);

  // Р”РѕР±Р°РІР»РµРЅРѕ: Р›РѕРіРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёРµ РїСЂРё РёРЅРёС†РёР°Р»РёР·Р°С†РёРё С…СѓРєР°
  console.log('=== useSituation HOOK STARTED ===');
  console.log('sitId from URL:', sitId);
  console.log('nodeId from URL:', nodeId);
  console.log('typeof sitId:', typeof sitId);
  console.log('sitId length:', sitId?.length);

  // Р¤СѓРЅРєС†РёСЏ РґР»СЏ РїРѕРёСЃРєР° СЃРёС‚СѓР°С†РёРё РїРѕ СЂР°Р·РЅС‹Рј РєСЂРёС‚РµСЂРёСЏРј
  const findSituation = (id) => {
    if (!id) {
      console.log('findSituation: id is null or undefined');
      console.log('id value:', id);
      return null;
    }

    console.log(`=== findSituation START ===`);
    console.log(`findSituation: searching for "${id}"`);
    console.log('id type:', typeof id);
    console.log('id trimmed:', `"${id.trim()}"`);
    console.log('Available keys:', Object.keys(situationsData));

    // РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЏРµРј РєР°Р¶РґСѓСЋ СЃРёС‚СѓР°С†РёСЋ
    console.log('=== Checking all situations ===');
    Object.entries(situationsData).forEach(([key, sit]) => {
      console.log(`Checking: key="${key}", sit.id="${sit.id}", title="${sit.title}"`);
      console.log(`  key === id? ${key === id}`);
      console.log(`  key === id.trim()? ${key === id.trim()}`);
      console.log(`  sit.id === id? ${sit.id === id}`);
      console.log(`  sit.id === id.trim()? ${sit.id === id.trim()}`);
    });

    // 1. РџСЂСЏРјРѕРµ СЃРѕРІРїР°РґРµРЅРёРµ (СЃ С‚СЂРёРјРјРёРЅРіРѕРј)
    const trimmedId = id.trim();
    if (situationsData[trimmedId]) {
      console.log(`вњ“ Found by direct match: "${trimmedId}"`);
      return { key: trimmedId, data: situationsData[trimmedId] };
    }

    // 2. РС‰РµРј РїРѕ РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРµРЅРЅРµРјСѓ ID (СЃ С‚СЂРёРјРјРёРЅРіРѕРј)
    for (const [key, sit] of Object.entries(situationsData)) {
      if (sit.id === trimmedId) {
        console.log(`вњ“ Found by internal id match: key="${key}", sit.id="${sit.id}"`);
        return { key, data: sit };
      }
    }

    // 3. РС‰РµРј РїРѕ Р·Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРєСѓ (С‡Р°СЃС‚РёС‡РЅРѕРµ СЃРѕРІРїР°РґРµРЅРёРµ) - Р±РµР· СѓС‡РµС‚Р° СЂРµРіРёСЃС‚СЂР°
    for (const [key, sit] of Object.entries(situationsData)) {
      if (sit.title?.toLowerCase().includes(trimmedId.toLowerCase())) {
        console.log(`вњ“ Found by title match: "${sit.title}" includes "${trimmedId}"`);
        return { key, data: sit };
      }
    }

    // 4. РС‰РµРј Р»СЋР±С‹Рµ СЃРѕРІРїР°РґРµРЅРёСЏ РІ РєР»СЋС‡Рµ РёР»Рё ID
    for (const [key, sit] of Object.entries(situationsData)) {
      if (key.toLowerCase().includes(trimmedId.toLowerCase())) {
        console.log(`вњ“ Found by key partial match: key "${key}" includes "${trimmedId}"`);
        return { key, data: sit };
      }
    }

    // 5. Р‘РµСЂРµРј РїРµСЂРІСѓСЋ РґРѕСЃС‚СѓРїРЅСѓСЋ СЃРёС‚СѓР°С†РёСЋ (С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РґР»СЏ РѕС‚Р»Р°РґРєРё)
    const firstKey = Object.keys(situationsData)[0];
    if (firstKey && trimmedId === 'debug') {
      console.log('вљ пёЏ Using first available situation for debugging');
      return { key: firstKey, data: situationsData[firstKey] };
    }

    console.log(`вњ— No situation found for "${id}"`);
    console.log(`=== findSituation END ===`);
    return null;
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    const loadSituation = async () => {
      setLoading(true);
      setError(null);

      try {
        console.log('=== LOADING SITUATION START ===');
        console.log('URL parameters - sitId:', sitId);
        console.log('URL parameters - nodeId:', nodeId);
        console.log('sitId type:', typeof sitId);
        console.log('Available situations:', Object.keys(situationsData));

        // Р”РµС‚Р°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ Р»РѕРі РІСЃРµС… СЃРёС‚СѓР°С†РёР№
        if (Object.keys(situationsData).length === 0) {
          console.error('ERROR: situationsData is EMPTY!');
        } else {
          console.log('=== All available situations ===');
          Object.entries(situationsData).forEach(([key, sit], index) => {
            console.log(`${index + 1}. Key: "${key}"`);
            console.log(`   ID: "${sit.id}"`);
            console.log(`   Title: "${sit.title}"`);
            console.log(`   Initial node: "${sit.initialNode}"`);
          });
        }

        if (!sitId) {
          console.error('ERROR: sitId is not provided');
          console.error('Current URL:', window.location.href);
          setError('ID СЃРёС‚СѓР°С†РёРё РЅРµ СѓРєР°Р·Р°РЅ. РџРѕР¶Р°Р»СѓР№СЃС‚Р°, РІС‹Р±РµСЂРёС‚Рµ СЃРёС‚СѓР°С†РёСЋ РёР· РєР°С‚Р°Р»РѕРіР°.');
          setLoading(false);
          return;
        }

        if (sitId === 'undefined' || sitId === 'null') {
          console.error(`ERROR: Invalid sitId value: "${sitId}"`);
          setError(`РќРµРєРѕСЂСЂРµРєС‚РЅС‹Р№ ID СЃРёС‚СѓР°С†РёРё: "${sitId}". Р’РµСЂРЅРёС‚РµСЃСЊ РІ РєР°С‚Р°Р»РѕРі Рё РІС‹Р±РµСЂРёС‚Рµ СЃРёС‚СѓР°С†РёСЋ Р·Р°РЅРѕРІРѕ.`);
          setLoading(false);
          return;
        }

        // РС‰РµРј СЃРёС‚СѓР°С†РёСЋ
        const found = findSituation(sitId);

        if (!found) {
          console.error('ERROR: Situation not found for ID:', sitId);
          console.error('Searched ID:', sitId);
          console.error('Available IDs:', Object.keys(situationsData));

          // РџСЂРµРґР»Р°РіР°РµРј РІРѕР·РјРѕР¶РЅС‹Рµ РІР°СЂРёР°РЅС‚С‹
          const suggestions = Object.keys(situationsData).filter(key =>
            key.toLowerCase().includes(sitId.toLowerCase()) ||
            situationsData[key].title.toLowerCase().includes(sitId.toLowerCase())
          );

          if (suggestions.length > 0) {
            console.log('Did you mean one of these?', suggestions);
            setError(`РЎРёС‚СѓР°С†РёСЏ "${sitId}" РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅР°. Р’РѕР·РјРѕР¶РЅРѕ, РІС‹ РёРјРµР»Рё РІ РІРёРґСѓ: ${suggestions.map(s => `"${s}"`).join(', ')}`);
          } else {
            setError(`РЎРёС‚СѓР°С†РёСЏ "${sitId}" РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅР°. Р”РѕСЃС‚СѓРїРЅС‹Рµ СЃРёС‚СѓР°С†РёРё: ${Object.keys(situationsData).join(', ')}`);
          }

          setLoading(false);
          return;
        }

        const { key, data } = found;
        console.log('вњ… Found situation:', key);
        console.log('   Title:', data.title);
        console.log('   ID:', data.id);
        console.log('   Initial node:', data.initialNode);
        console.log('   Available nodes:', Object.keys(data.nodes || {}));

        setSituation(data);

        // РћРїСЂРµРґРµР»СЏРµРј С‚РµРєСѓС‰РёР№ СѓР·РµР»
        const initialNode = nodeId || data.initialNode;
        console.log('Selected node:', initialNode);

        const node = data.nodes[initialNode];

        if (!node) {
          console.error(`ERROR: Node "${initialNode}" not found in situation "${key}"`);
          console.error('Available nodes:', Object.keys(data.nodes));
          setError(`РЈР·РµР» "${initialNode}" РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅ. Р”РѕСЃС‚СѓРїРЅС‹Рµ СѓР·Р»С‹: ${Object.keys(data.nodes).join(', ')}`);
          setLoading(false);
          return;
        }

        console.log('вњ… Loaded node:', initialNode);
        console.log('   Node description length:', node.description?.length || 0);
        console.log('   Has choices?', !!node.choices);
        console.log('   Has outcome?', !!node.outcomeRef);

        if (node.choices) {
          console.log('   Choices:', node.choices.map(c => `${c.id}: ${c.text || c.description}`));
        }

        setCurrentNode(node);
        setChoiceHistory([initialNode]);

        console.log('=== SITUATION LOADED SUCCESSFULLY ===');
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('ERROR loading situation:', err);
        console.error('Error stack:', err.stack);
        setError(`РћС€РёР±РєР° Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєРё: ${err.message}. РџРѕР¶Р°Р»СѓР№СЃС‚Р°, РїРѕРїСЂРѕР±СѓР№С‚Рµ СЃРЅРѕРІР° РёР»Рё РІС‹Р±РµСЂРёС‚Рµ РґСЂСѓРіСѓСЋ СЃРёС‚СѓР°С†РёСЋ.`);
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    };

    loadSituation();
  }, [sitId, nodeId]);

  const getContext = useCallback(() => {
    if (!situation || !libraryData.contexts) {
      console.log('getContext: situation or contexts not available');
      return null;
    }

    const context = libraryData.contexts[situation.contextRef];
    console.log('getContext: ref=', situation.contextRef, 'found=', !!context);
    return context || null;
  }, [situation]);

  const getProblem = useCallback(() => {
    if (!situation || !libraryData.problems) {
      console.log('getProblem: situation or problems not available');
      return null;
    }

    const problem = libraryData.problems[situation.problemRef];
    console.log('getProblem: ref=', situation.problemRef, 'found=', !!problem);
    return problem || null;
  }, [situation]);

  const getParticipants = useCallback(() => {
    if (!situation || !libraryData.participants) {
      console.log('getParticipants: situation or participants not available');
      return [];
    }

    const participants = situation.participantsRef
      .map(ref => {
        const participant = libraryData.participants[ref];
        console.log('getParticipants: ref=', ref, 'found=', !!participant);
        return participant;
      })
      .filter(Boolean);

    console.log('getParticipants: total found=', participants.length);
    return participants;
  }, [situation]);

  const getOutcome = useCallback((outcomeRef) => {
    if (!libraryData.outcomes) {
      console.log('getOutcome: outcomes not available');
      return null;
    }

    const outcome = libraryData.outcomes[outcomeRef];
    console.log('getOutcome: ref=', outcomeRef, 'found=', !!outcome);
    return outcome || null;
  }, []);

  const getSolution = useCallback((solutionRef) => {
    if (!libraryData.solutions) {
      console.log('getSolution: solutions not available');
      return null;
    }

    const solution = libraryData.solutions[solutionRef];
    console.log('getSolution: ref=', solutionRef, 'found=', !!solution);
    return solution || null;
  }, []);

  const makeChoice = useCallback((choice) => {
    if (!choice || !situation) {
      console.error('makeChoice: choice or situation not available');
      return;
    }

    console.log('=== MAKING CHOICE ===');
    console.log('Choice ID:', choice.id);
    console.log('Choice text:', choice.text || choice.description);
    console.log('Next node:', choice.next);
    console.log('Available nodes:', Object.keys(situation.nodes));

    const nextNode = situation.nodes[choice.next];
    if (!nextNode) {
      console.error(`ERROR: РЈР·РµР» "${choice.next}" РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅ`);
      console.error('Available nodes:', Object.keys(situation.nodes));
      return;
    }

    console.log('вњ… Next node found');
    setCurrentNode(nextNode);
    setChoiceHistory(prev => [...prev, choice.next]);

    // РћР±РЅРѕРІР»СЏРµРј URL
    navigate(`/situation/${sitId}/${choice.next}`, { replace: false });
    console.log('Navigated to:', `/situation/${sitId}/${choice.next}`);
  }, [situation, sitId, navigate]);

  const restartSituation = useCallback(() => {
    if (!situation) {
      console.error('restartSituation: situation not available');
      return;
    }

    console.log('=== RESTARTING SITUATION ===');
    const initialNode = situation.initialNode;
    const node = situation.nodes[initialNode];

    if (node) {
      console.log('Restarting to initial node:', initialNode);
      setCurrentNode(node);
      setChoiceHistory([initialNode]);
      navigate(`/situation/${sitId}/${initialNode}`);
    } else {
      console.error('Cannot restart: initial node not found', initialNode);
    }
  }, [situation, sitId, navigate]);

  const goBack = useCallback(() => {
    if (choiceHistory.length <= 1) {
      console.log('goBack: cannot go back, history length <= 1');
      return;
    }

    console.log('=== GOING BACK ===');
    const newHistory = [...choiceHistory];
    newHistory.pop();
    const prevNodeId = newHistory[newHistory.length - 1];

    console.log('Previous history:', choiceHistory);
    console.log('New history:', newHistory);
    console.log('Previous node ID:', prevNodeId);

    const prevNode = situation.nodes[prevNodeId];
    if (prevNode) {
      console.log('вњ… Previous node found');
      setCurrentNode(prevNode);
      setChoiceHistory(newHistory);
      navigate(`/situation/${sitId}/${prevNodeId}`);
    } else {
      console.error('Cannot go back: previous node not found', prevNodeId);
    }
  }, [choiceHistory, situation, sitId, navigate]);

  // Р”РѕР±Р°РІР»РµРЅРѕ: Р›РѕРіРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёРµ СЃРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏРЅРёСЏ РїСЂРё РІРѕР·РІСЂР°С‚Рµ
  console.log('=== useSituation HOOK STATE ===');
  console.log('loading:', loading);
  console.log('error:', error);
  console.log('situation:', situation ? situation.title : 'null');
  console.log('currentNode:', currentNode ? currentNode.id : 'null');
  console.log('choiceHistory:', choiceHistory);
  console.log('canGoBack:', choiceHistory.length > 1);
  console.log('sitId:', sitId);

  return {
    situation,
    currentNode,
    history: choiceHistory,
    loading,
    error,
    getContext,
    getProblem,
    getParticipants,
    getOutcome,
    getSolution,
    makeChoice,
    restartSituation,
    goBack,
    canGoBack: choiceHistory.length > 1,
    sitId
  };
};

    padding: 8px 16px;
    font-size: 0.85rem;
  }

  .no-results i {
    font-size: 3rem;
  }

  .no-results h3 {
    font-size: 1.5rem;
  }
}

@media (max-width: 480px) {
  .filter-buttons {
    justify-content: space-between;
  }

  .filter-btn {
    flex: 1;
    min-width: 120px;
    text-align: center;
  }
}

// src/pages/Catalog.jsx
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import Header from '../components/Header/Header';
import Footer from '../components/Footer/Footer';
import ScenarioCard from '../components/ScenarioCard/ScenarioCard';
import './Catalog.css';

const Catalog = () => {
  const [typeFilter, setTypeFilter] = useState('all');
  const [difficultyFilter, setDifficultyFilter] = useState('all');
  const [filteredScenarios, setFilteredScenarios] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);
  const [serverScenarios, setServerScenarios] = useState([]);

  // 1. Р¤СѓРЅРєС†РёСЏ РґР»СЏ Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєРё РґР°РЅРЅС‹С… СЃ СЃРµСЂРІРµСЂР°
  const fetchScenarios = async () => {
    try {
      console.log('рџ”„ Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР¶Р°СЋ СЃС†РµРЅР°СЂРёРё СЃ СЃРµСЂРІРµСЂР°...');
      setLoading(true);

      const response = await fetch('http://localhost:5000/api/situations');

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРµСЂРІРµСЂР°: ${response.status}`);
      }

      const data = await response.json();
      console.log('вњ… Р”Р°РЅРЅС‹Рµ РїРѕР»СѓС‡РµРЅС‹:', data);

      // Р”Р°РЅРЅС‹Рµ СЃ СЃРµСЂРІРµСЂР° СѓР¶Рµ РІ РїСЂР°РІРёР»СЊРЅРѕРј С„РѕСЂРјР°С‚Рµ!
      setServerScenarios(data);
      setFilteredScenarios(data);
      setError(null);

    } catch (err) {
      console.error('вќЊ РћС€РёР±РєР° Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєРё:', err);
      setError('РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РёС‚СЊ РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ СЃ СЃРµСЂРІРµСЂР°');
      // РСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµРј Р»РѕРєР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ РєР°Рє fallback
      const localData = getLocalScenarios();
      setServerScenarios(localData);
      setFilteredScenarios(localData);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // 2. Р›РѕРєР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ (С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РєР°Рє fallback)
  const getLocalScenarios = () => {
    return [
      {
        id: 1,
        title: "В«Р­РєСЂР°РЅ РІР°Р¶РЅРµРµ Р°Р»РіРѕСЂРёС‚РјРѕРІВ»: СѓС‡РµРЅРёРє РїРѕРіСЂСѓР¶РµРЅ РІ С‚РµР»РµС„РѕРЅ",
        description: "РљР°Рє РІРµСЂРЅСѓС‚СЊ РІРЅРёРјР°РЅРёРµ Рє СѓСЂРѕРєСѓ, РЅРµ СЂР°Р·СЂСѓС€Р°СЏ РґРѕРІРµСЂРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РѕС‚РЅРѕС€РµРЅРёСЏ?",
        difficulty: "hard",
        type: "РњРѕС‚РёРІР°С†РёРѕРЅРЅС‹Рµ РєСЂРёР·РёСЃС‹",
        duration: 10,
        typeIcon: "lightbulb",
        dataType: "motivational"
      },
      {
        id: 2,
        title: "В«РЇ РЅРµ СѓСЃРїРµРІР°СЋ, Р° РѕРЅ СѓР¶Рµ РІСЃС‘ СЃРґРµР»Р°Р»В»",
        description: "РљР°Рє РѕСЂРіР°РЅРёР·РѕРІР°С‚СЊ СѓСЂРѕРє, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ РЅРµ РґРµРјРѕС‚РёРІРёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ РЅРё СЃРёР»СЊРЅС‹С…, РЅРё СЃР»Р°Р±С‹С…?",
        difficulty: "medium",
        type: "РћСЂРіР°РЅРёР·Р°С†РёРѕРЅРЅС‹Рµ РєРѕРЅС„Р»РёРєС‚С‹",
        duration: 10,
        typeIcon: "tasks",
        dataType: "organizational"
      },
      {
        id: 3,
        title: "В«РЁР°Р№Р±Р° РіРЅРµРІР°В»: С…РѕРєРєРµРёСЃС‚ РїСЂРѕС‚РёРІ В«РЅРµСЃРїСЂР°РІРµРґР»РёРІРѕСЃС‚РёВ» СѓС‡РёС‚РµР»СЏ",
        description: "РљР°Рє РїРµСЂРµРІРµСЃС‚Рё Р°РіСЂРµСЃСЃРёСЋ СЃРїРѕСЂС‚РёРІРЅРѕРіРѕ Р»РёРґРµСЂР° РІ РєРѕРЅСЃС‚СЂСѓРєС‚РёРІРЅРѕРµ СЂСѓСЃР»Рѕ, РЅРµ С‚РµСЂСЏСЏ Р°РІС‚РѕСЂРёС‚РµС‚ РїРµСЂРµРґ РєР»Р°СЃСЃРѕРј?",
        difficulty: "easy",
        type: "РљРѕРЅС„Р»РёРєС‚С‹ РІР·Р°РёРјРѕРґРµР№СЃС‚РІРёСЏ",
        duration: 8,
        typeIcon: "comments",
        dataType: "interaction"
      }
    ];
  };

  // 3. Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР¶Р°РµРј РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ РїСЂРё РјРѕРЅС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёРё РєРѕРјРїРѕРЅРµРЅС‚Р°
  useEffect(() => {
    fetchScenarios();
  }, []);

  // 4. Р¤РёР»СЊС‚СЂР°С†РёСЏ СЃС†РµРЅР°СЂРёРµРІ
  useEffect(() => {
    const filtered = serverScenarios.filter(scenario => {
      // РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєР° СЃР»РѕР¶РЅРѕСЃС‚Рё
      const difficultyMatch = difficultyFilter === 'all' ||
        scenario.difficulty === difficultyFilter;

      // РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєР° С‚РёРїР°
      let typeMatch = false;
      if (typeFilter === 'all') {
        typeMatch = true;
      } else if (scenario.dataType && scenario.dataType.includes(' ')) {
        // Р”Р»СЏ РґРІРѕР№РЅС‹С… С‚РёРїРѕРІ РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЏРµРј РєР°Р¶РґС‹Р№ (РЅР°РїСЂРёРјРµСЂ: "organizational interaction")
        const types = scenario.dataType.split(' ');
        typeMatch = types.includes(typeFilter);
      } else {
        typeMatch = scenario.dataType === typeFilter;
      }

      return difficultyMatch && typeMatch;
    });

    setFilteredScenarios(filtered);
  }, [typeFilter, difficultyFilter, serverScenarios]);

  // 5. РўРёРїС‹ СЃРёС‚СѓР°С†РёР№ РґР»СЏ С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂРѕРІ
  const situationTypes = [
    { value: 'all', label: 'Р’СЃРµ' },
    { value: 'motivational', label: 'РњРѕС‚РёРІР°С†РёРѕРЅРЅС‹Рµ РєСЂРёР·РёСЃС‹' },
    { value: 'organizational', label: 'РћСЂРіР°РЅРёР·Р°С†РёРѕРЅРЅС‹Рµ РєРѕРЅС„Р»РёРєС‚С‹' },
    { value: 'interaction', label: 'РљРѕРЅС„Р»РёРєС‚С‹ РІР·Р°РёРјРѕРґРµР№СЃС‚РІРёСЏ' },
    { value: 'intrapersonal', label: 'Р’РЅСѓС‚СЂРёР»РёС‡РЅРѕСЃС‚РЅС‹Рµ РєРѕРЅС„Р»РёРєС‚С‹' },
    { value: 'interpersonal', label: 'РњРµР¶Р»РёС‡РЅРѕСЃС‚РЅС‹Рµ РєРѕРЅС„Р»РёРєС‚С‹' },
    { value: 'person-vs-group', label: 'Р›РёС‡РЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ vs. РіСЂСѓРїРїР°' },
    { value: 'intergroup', label: 'РњРµР¶РіСЂСѓРїРїРѕРІС‹Рµ РєРѕРЅС„Р»РёРєС‚С‹' }
  ];

  // 6. РЈСЂРѕРІРЅРё СЃР»РѕР¶РЅРѕСЃС‚Рё
  const difficultyLevels = [
    { value: 'all', label: 'Р’СЃРµ' },
    { value: 'easy', label: 'Р‘Р°Р·РѕРІС‹Р№' },
    { value: 'medium', label: 'РЎСЂРµРґРЅРёР№' },
    { value: 'hard', label: 'РџСЂРѕРґРІРёРЅСѓС‚С‹Р№' }
  ];

  // 7. Р¤СѓРЅРєС†РёСЏ РґР»СЏ РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅРёСЏ РґР°РЅРЅС‹С…
  const handleRefresh = () => {
    fetchScenarios();
  };

  return (
    <>
      <Header />

      {/* Р“РµСЂРѕР№ СЃРµРєС†РёСЏ РєР°С‚Р°Р»РѕРіР° */}
      <section className="catalog-hero">
        <div className="container">
          <h2>РљР°С‚Р°Р»РѕРі РїРµРґР°РіРѕРіРёС‡РµСЃРєРёС… СЃРёС‚СѓР°С†РёР№</h2>
          <p className="hero-desc">
            {serverScenarios.length} СЃС†РµРЅР°СЂРёРµРІ, СЃС‚СЂСѓРєС‚СѓСЂРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРЅС‹С… РїРѕ РѕРЅС‚РѕР»РѕРіРёС‡РµСЃРєРѕР№ РєР»Р°СЃСЃРёС„РёРєР°С†РёРё РїРµРґР°РіРѕРіРёС‡РµСЃРєРёС… СЃРёС‚СѓР°С†РёР№.
            РљР°Р¶РґР°СЏ СЃРёС‚СѓР°С†РёСЏ вЂ” СЌС‚Рѕ С€Р°Рі Рє РѕСЃРѕР·РЅР°РЅРЅРѕРјСѓ, СЌС‚РёС‡РЅРѕРјСѓ Рё СЃС‚СЂР°С‚РµРіРёС‡РµСЃРєРё РІС‹РІРµСЂРµРЅРЅРѕРјСѓ СЂРµС€РµРЅРёСЋ.
          </p>

          {/* РџР°РЅРµР»СЊ СѓРїСЂР°РІР»РµРЅРёСЏ Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєРѕР№ */}
          <div className="server-controls">
            <button
              onClick={handleRefresh}
              className="refresh-btn"
              disabled={loading}
